This is a pipelining issue - the comparison should probably check saved_sample_cnt vs framesize_d, but since saved_sample_cnt is also updated in the same cycle, there's a 1-cycle delay issue.

    Actually wait - in VHDL, signal assignments don't take effect until the end of the process. So `saved_sample_cnt_d <= saved_sample_cnt;` means saved_sample_cnt_d will get the current value of saved_sample_cnt at the end of the process. The comparison `saved_sample_cnt_d = unsigned(framesize_d)` uses the OLD value of saved_sample_cnt_d (from the previous cycle). And `saved_sample_cnt <= saved_sample_cnt + 1` updates saved_sample_cnt to the new value, but this won't be visible until the next clock cycle.

    So the flow is:
    - Cycle N: saved_sample_cnt = X, saved_sample_cnt_d = X-1 (from previous cycle)
    - Comparison: saved_sample_cnt_d (= X-1) == framesize_d? 
    - If not: saved_sample_cnt becomes X+1, saved_sample_cnt_d becomes X
    - Cycle N+1: saved_sample_cnt = X+1, saved_sample_cnt_d = X
    - Comparison: saved_sample_cnt_d (= X) == framesize_d?

    So when saved_sample_cnt reaches framesize_d+1, saved_sample_cnt_d will be framesize_d, and the comparison will match. This means we'll write one extra sample beyond framesize_d. This is a off-by-one issue.

    Actually, let me think more carefully. Initially saved_sample_cnt = 0 and saved_sample_cnt_d = 0.

    Cycle 1 (sampling_CE = '1', ADC_E):
    - Compare saved_sample_cnt_d (=0) == framesize_d? Assume not
    - DataWriteEn <= '1'
    - saved_sample_cnt <= 0 + 1 = 1
    - saved_sample_cnt_d <= 0

    Cycle 2:
    - saved_sample_cnt = 1, saved_sample_cnt_d = 0
    - Compare saved_sample_cnt_d (=0) == framesize_d? If framesize_d = 10000, then no
    - saved_sample_cnt <= 2
    - saved_sample_cnt_d <= 1
    
    ...
    
    When saved_sample_cnt = framesize_d (=10000):
    - We're in a cycle where saved_sample_cnt was just updated to 10000
    - But the comparison uses saved_sample_cnt_d which is 9999 (from previous cycle)
    - So comparison says: 9999 == 10000? No
    - saved_sample_cnt <= 10001
    - saved_sample_cnt_d <= 10000

    Next cycle:
    - saved_sample_cnt = 10001, saved_sample_cnt_d = 10000
    - Comparison: 10000 == 10000? YES
    - DataWriteEn <= '0'
    - t_start <= '1'
    - Go to ADC_F

    So this writes framesize_d + 1 samples (indices 0 through 10000 = 10001 samples). Since framesize_d was stored as `unsigned(framesize) - 1` in the config read, this might be by design (framesize at register 9 stores value-1).

    Actually, looking at register 9: `framesize <= std_logic_vector(unsigned(cfg_do_B(26 downto 0))-1);` so framesize internally is (requested - 1). And then framesize_d takes that value. So if PC requests 10001 samples, framesize = 10000, and the state machine actually writes 10001 samples (0 to 10000). This seems correct actually.

29. **自动触发设计中存在的问题**
    - 在 ADC_C 状态，`auto_trigger_cnt` 会在监控触发条件的同时持续计数。
    - 在 ADC_D 状态，继续使用 `auto_trigger_cnt` 进行计数，但同时使用 `auto_trigger_d` 来指示超时。
    - 当进入 ADC_E 后，计数器不再递增，这意味着如果在 ADC_C 中计数器已经达到最大值，auto_trigger 会被设置。但如果从 ADC_C 直接转到 ADC_E（通过正常触发或立即触发），auto_trigger 可能为 0。

    但是主要问题是：在 ADC_C 中，如果触发条件满足立即转到 ADC_D 或 ADC_E，auto_trigger_cnt 不再递增。但如果 auto_trigger_cnt 在 ADC_C 中已经达到 auto_trigger_maxcnt，则 auto_trigger <= '1'，而在 ADC_D 中使用 auto_trigger_d。如果在 ADC_C 中 auto_trigger 变 '1'，但在进入 ADC_D 前触发了正常触发条件（转到 ADC_E），auto_trigger 不会复位。

    Actually, auto_trigger 在 ADC_A 中被复位了，所以当新的帧开始时它被清零。并且每次进入 ADC_C 时，auto_trigger_cnt 从 reset 值开始（在 ADC_A 中被重置为 0）。所以问题应该是：一旦 auto_trigger 被设置，它会在同一个帧的 ADC_C 或 ADC_D 中被检查，一旦进入 ADC_E 就不再使用。这看起来是正常的。

30. **内存代码中 `requestFrame_dd` 的声明缺失**
    - `requestFrame_dd` 在 `ADC_interface_rising` 进程中被使用：
      ```vhdl
      requestFrame_d <= requestFrame;    
      requestFrame_dd <= requestFrame_d;
      if requestFrame_dd = '0' AND requestFrame_d = '1' then
          getNewFrame <= '1';
      end if;
      ```
    - 但是在信号声明区域只有 `requestFrame_d` 和 `requestFrame` 被声明，没有 `requestFrame_dd`。

31. **`getNewFrame` vs `getnewframe` 大小写问题**
    - 在代码中，`getNewFrame`（大写的 N 和 F）被使用：
      - 在 ADC 进程：`if ( getNewFrame = '1'...`
      - `getNewFrame <= '1';`
    - 但 CDC 同步链用的是 `getnewframe`（全小写）：
      - `getnewframe_d <= getnewframe;`
      
    这两个在 VHDL 中是大小写不敏感的，所以实际上是同一个信号。但风格上很不一致，可能混淆阅读者。

32. **`ScopeConfigChanged` vs `scopeConfigChanged` 大小写不一致**
    - 在状态 B 中：`if scopeConfigChanged = '1' then` 和 `scopeConfigChanged <= '0';`
    - 但信号声明是 `ScopeConfigChanged`（大写 S）
    
    在 VHDL 中大小写不敏感，所以这是同一个信号，但导致严重的可读性问题。

33. **ADC SPI 写触发问题**
    - 在状态 D 中：
      ```vhdl
      if ConfigureADC = '1' OR adcA_spi_busy = '1' then
          adc_spi_data <= adc_cfg_reg & adc_cfg_data;
          ConfigureADC <= '0';
          Masterstate <= D;
      else
          Masterstate <= B;
      end if;
      ```
    - 当 `ConfigureADC = '1'` 时，它立即将 `ConfigureADC` 置为 '0'。但需要注意的是，SPI 模块的 `spi_write_trig` 是直接连接到 `ConfigureADC` 的。如果 `ConfigureADC` 只保持了一个周期的高电平，那么 SPI 模块可能无法正确检测到触发脉冲，因为 `spi_write_trig` 的触发条件可能是电平敏感的或者边沿敏感的。
    
    实际上，SPI 模块通过 `spi_write_trig` 触发，如果它是边沿敏感的，那么 ConfigureADC 在状态 A 中被置为 '1'，然后在下一个 ifclk 周期进入状态 D 时立即被清零。如果 SPI 是在 ifclk 的同一个时钟域工作，它应该能检测到这一个周期的脉冲。

    但是状态 A 中 `ConfigureADC <= '1'` 和 `MasterState <= A; Timer_cnt <= Timer_cnt + 1;` 是同时执行的。然后在下一个周期，因为 MasterState 还是 A，Timer_cnt 更新了，所以条件 `elsif Timer_cnt = 40000 then` 等条件不再满足，进入 `else` 分支：`ConfigureADC <= '0'`。所以 ConfigureADC 只保持了一个周期的高电平，这是正确的脉冲行为。

    但在状态 D 中，进入时就执行 `ConfigureADC <= '0'`，SPI 模块是否能检测到这个脉冲取决于时序。在 VHDL 中，信号赋值在进程结束时才生效，所以状态 D 中的 `ConfigureADC <= '0'` 要到进程结束时才生效，SPI 模块在同一个时钟沿看到的还是 ConfigureADC = '1'。所以脉冲宽度是 1 个时钟周期，这是正确的。

    Actually wait - in state A, the sequence is:
    1. Timer_cnt = 40000
    2. `adc_spi_data <= ...; ConfigureADC <= '1'; MasterState <= A; Timer_cnt <= Timer_cnt + 1;`
    
    But this means Timer_cnt becomes 40001 and MasterState stays A. On the next clock cycle:
    1. Timer_cnt = 40001
    2. None of the specific conditions match (40000, 40100, etc.)
    3. Goes to else: `ConfigureADC <= '0'; MasterState <= A; Timer_cnt <= Timer_cnt + 1;`
    
    So ConfigureADC is high for exactly one cycle in state A. Then goes to state D:
    - In state D: `ConfigureADC <= '0';` is executed again.
    
    OK this seems fine for a 1-cycle pulse.

34. **LED 驱动问题**
    - `LED(3) <= LED_i(3) OR NOT(init_calib_complete_d);`
    - 但是 `LED_i(3)` 只被 `trigger_led_blink` 实例驱动。LED(1) 和 LED(2) 的 `LED_i(1)` 和 `LED_i(2)` 没有被赋值（保持初始值 "000"），所以 LED(1) 和 LED(2) 只反映校准状态。

35. **未声明的 `clk_adc_dclk` 用于 PLL 输入**
    - `clk_wiz_0_pll` 的 `clk_in1` 连接到 `clk_adc_dclk`，这是 ADC 接口恢复的时钟。但 `clk_adc_dclk` 是在 ADC 校准完成前可能不稳定的。

36. **`slrd_sloe` 同时驱动 SLRD 和 SLOE**
    - 注释说"读控制（SLOE与SLRD并联）"，但这意味着这两个功能被合并了。在某些 FX3 时序模式下这可以工作，但不是所有模式都支持。

37. **`pll_reset` 在 ifclk 域中控制，但 PLL 使用 clk_adc_dclk 作为输入**
    - PLL 复位逻辑在 `FX3_interface` 进程（ifclk 域）中，但 PLL 实例的时钟输入是 clk_adc_dclk。如果 PLL 的复位跨时钟域，可能存在 CDC 问题。

38. **`mavg_enA_d` 和 `mavg_enB_d` 的赋值时机**
    - 在 ADC_A 状态中：`mavg_enA_d <= mavg_enA;`
    - 但 `mavg_enA` 在 ADC 域的配置读取中更新（也在同一个进程中）。
    - 由于在采样使能门控 (`sampling_CE = '1'`) 内，这限制了更新频率，但可能存在在使能脉冲之间配置变化无法及时传递的问题。

39. **`post_trigger` 信号声明但计算被注释掉**
    - `signal post_trigger : UNSIGNED (26 downto 0);` 已声明
    - 在 ADC_B 中：`--post_trigger <= unsigned(framesize_d) - pre_trigger_d + 1;` 被注释

40. **`DAC_pogramming_start` 拼写错误** - 应该是 `DAC_programming_start`

## 🔴 严重问题（功能可能异常）

### 1. 状态 B — `TEST_FRAME_MODE` if 块重复三遍（第 1555–1595 行附近）
```vhdl
if TEST_FRAME_MODE and flaga_d = '1' then  -- 完全相同的内容出现3次
```
同一个条件下对 `faddr_i`、`hword_cnt_i`、`slwr_assert_cnt`、`test_frame_cnt`、`Masterstate` 同时赋值。VHDL 中多个驱动源对同一信号赋值会引发**多驱动源冲突**，综合时会报错或产生不可预测行为。

### 2. `requestFrame_dd` 信号未声明
在 `ADC_interface_rising` 进程中使用了：
```vhdl
requestFrame_dd <= requestFrame_d;
if requestFrame_dd = '0' AND requestFrame_d = '1' then
```
但信号声明区只有 `requestFrame` 和 `requestFrame_d`，缺少 `requestFrame_dd`。综合会报 **Signal not declared** 错误。

### 3. `getNewFrame` vs `getnewframe` 大小写不一致
- ADC 进程（`clk_adc_dclk` 域）中使用的信号是 `getNewFrame`（大写 NF）
- FX3 进程（`ifclk` 域）中的 CDC 同步链用的是 `getnewframe`（全小写）

VHDL 大小写不敏感，所以本质上是同一个信号。**但**这意味着：
- 信号声明区只有 `getnewframe`（全小写），`getNewFrame` 是同一个信号
- 代码阅读上极其混乱，容易以为这是两个不同的信号

### 4. `ScopeConfigChanged` vs `scopeConfigChanged` 大小写不一致
信号声明为 `ScopeConfigChanged`（大写 S），但状态 B 中写的是：
```vhdl
if scopeConfigChanged = '1' then   -- 小写 s
    scopeConfigChanged <= '0';
```
VHDL 大小写不敏感，这能综合，但**风格极差**，严重影响可维护性。

### 5. 双边沿触发确认逻辑异常（ADC_D 状态）
```vhdl
elsif trigger_source_d < "010" AND trigger_slope_d = "10"
       AND ( abs(trig_signal_d) >= trig_level_r_dd ) then
```
双边沿用 `abs()` 做绝对值比较，这与上升沿（`>= trig_level_r_dd`）和下降沿（`< trig_level_f_dd`）的逻辑**不对称**。双边沿的正确做法应是：
```vhdl
(trig_signal_d >= trig_level_r_dd) OR (trig_signal_d < trig_level_f_dd)
```
当前绝对值逻辑可能在某些电平下漏触发或误触发。

---

## 🟠 中等问题

### 6. `saved_sample_cnt` / `saved_sample_cnt_d` 比较时序问题
在 ADC_E 状态中：
```vhdl
if ( saved_sample_cnt_d = unsigned(framesize_d) ) then  -- 使用旧值比较
    ...
else
    DataWriteEn <= '1';
end if;
saved_sample_cnt <= saved_sample_cnt + 1;
saved_sample_cnt_d <= saved_sample_cnt;  -- 当前拍更新，下拍才生效
```
`saved_sample_cnt_d` 始终滞后 `saved_sample_cnt` 一拍，导致判断退出条件时**多用一帧样本**。如果 `framesize_d` 是 `N-1` 格式（寄存器 9 做了减一），则实际写入 N+1 个样本，**多一个字的偏差**。

### 7. 状态 A 中 `read_calib_start` 只保持 1 拍但配置分两次发
```vhdl
elsif Timer_cnt = 30000 or Timer_cnt = 30001 then
    read_calib_start <= '1';
    read_calib_source <= '1';   -- CH2
```
在同一周期既设 `read_calib_start` 又设 `read_calib_source`，但 `read_calib_start` 在 else 分支中被清零。IDELAY 校准可能需要更宽的脉冲（adc_if 内部期待边沿触发还是电平触发？），如果边沿触发则 1 周期脉冲可能不够稳定。

### 8. `pll_reset` 跨时钟域控制
- PLL 复位逻辑在 `FX3_interface` 进程（`ifclk` 域）中产生
- 但 PLL 实例 `clk_wiz_0_pll` 的时钟输入是 `clk_adc_dclk`（ADC 时钟域）
- `pll_reset` 信号**没有做 CDC 同步**，可能导致 PLL 复位时序违规

### 9. `mavg_enA_d` / `mavg_enB_d` 在 sampling_CE 门控内赋值
```vhdl
if ( sampling_CE = '0' ) then
    ...  -- 不进入 state machine
else
    case ADC_A =>
        mavg_enA_d <= mavg_enA;  -- 在 sampling_CE = '1' 时更新
```
如果 `sampling_CE` 频率很低（慢时基），`mavg_enA_d` 可能长时间不更新，但 `mavg_enA` 可能在每个 ADC 时钟周期变化，导致 mavg_enA 的配置变化延迟生效。

---

## 🟡 轻微问题 / 代码异味

### 10. 大量声明的信号未使用（约 20+ 个）

|---|---|
| `flag_ep6_ready` | 声明未用 |
| `dataA`, `dataB` | 仅赋值未使用（调试残余） |
| `cnt_after_flagd` | 声明未用 |
| `s_trigger_rearm_completed` | 声明未用 |
| `adc_sclk_counter`, `adc_spi_bit_count`, `adc_configured_flag` | 声明未用 |

| `dora_i`, `dorb_i` | 声明未用 |

Vivado 综合时会产生数百条 **Unused signal** 警告。

### 11. `flaga_id` / `flaga_idd` 声明且有 ASYNC_REG 属性，但从未在逻辑中使用
声明为 CDC 同步寄存器，但实际的 `flaga` 同步只用 `flaga_d`，没有使用 `flaga_id/idd`。

### 12. `IEEE_PROPOSED.FIXED_PKG` 库被 use 但未使用
```vhdl
library IEEE_PROPOSED;
use IEEE_PROPOSED.FIXED_PKG.ALL;
```
代码中没有任何 `sfixed` 或 `ufixed` 类型的使用。`IEEE_PROPOSED` 不是标准 IEEE 库，某些综合工具可能不支持，且增加编译时间。

### 13. 状态 A 中的 LED 驱动
```vhdl
LED(1) <= LED_i(1) OR NOT(init_calib_complete_d);
LED(2) <= LED_i(2) OR NOT(init_calib_complete_d);
LED(3) <= LED_i(3) OR NOT(init_calib_complete_d);
```
但 `LED_i(1)` 和 `LED_i(2)` 从未被赋值（保持初始值 `'0'`），所以 LED(1) 和 LED(2) 实际上只反映 DDR3 校准状态，没有独立含义。

### 14. `DAC_pogramming_start` 拼写错误
应为 `DAC_programming_start`（遗漏了第一个 r）。

### 15. `MasterState` vs `Masterstate` 大小写不一致
状态机信号声明为 `MasterState`，但在状态 G 等多处使用 `Masterstate`（小写 s）。虽然 VHDL 不敏感，但极其不利于搜索和维护。

### 16. 配置 RAM 写使能 `cfg_we` 时序可能过窄
在状态 C 中，`cfg_we <= '1'` 和地址递增在同一拍生效，综合后 RAM 的写地址可能比数据先变化，导致写到错误地址。

### 17. `startup_timer_cnt` 的复位脉冲逻辑反转
```vhdl
if startup_timer_cnt = 4000 then
    gl_reset <= '0';
elsif startup_timer_cnt < 2000 then
    gl_reset <= '0';               -- 前半段低电平
    startup_timer_cnt <= startup_timer_cnt + 1;
else
    gl_reset <= '1';               -- 后半段高电平
    startup_timer_cnt <= startup_timer_cnt + 1;
end if;
```
`gl_reset` 先变 `'1'`（2000–3999 周期），然后到 4000 时变回 `'0'`（但不再递增计数器）。注意：`gl_reset` 用作 IDELAYCTRL 复位，**低有效还是高有效？** 如果 IDELAYCTRL 是低有效复位（实际 Xilinx 的 IDELAYCTRL 有 `RST` 端口，高有效），当前逻辑可能正确。但代码中 `gl_reset` 在第 4000 周期变成 `'0'` 后计数器不再变化，此后再也没有复位能力。如果 PLL 失锁后需要重新复位，这个机制不支持。

### 18. ADC 校准序列中 `read_calib_start` 在 else 里被强制清零
```vhdl
else
    read_calib_start <= '0';
```
在 `Timer_cnt = 20000 or 20001` 等条件下同时置 `read_calib_start <= '1'`，但下一周期如果 Timer_cnt 不匹配任何特定条件就进入 else 清零。这可能让校准脉冲宽度仅 1 周期，如果 adc_if 内部需要更宽的使能窗口则会失败。

Let me summarize what I've learned about the system:

1. **ScopeFun_core.vhd** - Main FPGA top-level for a USB oscilloscope
   - Uses FX3 (USB 3.0) interface for data transfer
   - AD9643 dual-channel ADC (14-bit) @ 250MHz DDR
   - DDR3 memory for sample buffer
   - Two PLL/clock domains: clk_adc_dclk (ADC sampling) and ifclk (DDR3 UI/FX3 transfer)
   - Main FSM: A (init) → B (dispatch) → C (read config) → D (ADC SPI) → E (DAC SPI) → F (wait frame) → G (stream data)
   - ADC sampling FSM: ADC_A (idle) → ADC_B (pre-trigger) → ADC_C (wait trigger) → ADC_D (confirm trigger) → ADC_E (post-trigger) → ADC_F (holdoff)
   - TEST_FRAME_MODE is set to TRUE - direct EP6 test, no ADC needed

2. **Key issues to fix**:
   - TEST_FRAME_MODE triple duplicate in state B
   - Missing signal declarations (requestFrame_dd)
   - Case inconsistency (ScopeConfigChanged, getNewFrame, Masterstate)

   - ADC_E off-by-one sample count
   - IEEE_PROPOSED.FIXED_PKG unused
   - Various code smells

3. **AD9643 info**:
   - The AD9643 is a 14-bit dual-channel ADC
   - 250 MHz DDR sampling clock
   - SPI register map: address byte + data byte, then transfer command 0xFF01
   - Current SPI sequences:
     a) Start with checkerboard test pattern (0x0D44) + transfer
     b) Read calib CH1 (IDELAY calibration using checkerboard)
     c) Read calib CH2
     d) Test pattern OFF (0x0D00) + transfer
     e) Normal operation

For 14-bit modifications:
- The ADC_BITS is already set to 14 in the component instantiation
- The data packing `data_in => std_logic_vector(dataAd) & std_logic_vector(dataBd) & (3 downto 0 => '0')` seems suspicious - it's concatenating two 14-bit signed values and then padding with zeros. This gives 14+14+4 = 32 bits. But dataAd and dataBd are signed(13 downto 0) which is 14 bits. 14 + 14 + 4 = 32. OK so that works.

Wait, let me re-check:
```vhdl
data_in => std_logic_vector(dataAd) & std_logic_vector(dataBd) & (3 downto 0 => '0'),
```
dataAd is SIGNED(13 downto 0) = 14 bits
dataBd is SIGNED(13 downto 0) = 14 bits
"0000" = 4 bits
Total: 14 + 14 + 4 = 32 bits. OK.

But wait - in the signal declarations, dataAd and dataBd are:
```vhdl
signal dataAd : SIGNED (13 downto 0);
signal dataBd : SIGNED (13 downto 0);
```
And adc_data_rise/fall are:
```vhdl
signal adc_data_rise : std_logic_vector(13 downto 0);
signal adc_data_fall : std_logic_vector(13 downto 0);
```

So 14 bits throughout. The concatenation is:
- bits 31:18 = dataAd (CH1 data, 14 bits)
- bits 17:4 = dataBd (CH2 data, 14 bits) 
- bits 3:0 = "0000" (padding)

This gives 14-bit CH1 and 14-bit CH2 data packed into 32 bits with 4 bits of padding. That seems correct.

For AD9643 control:
- The current SPI sequence in state A is correct for AD9643
- 0x0D = test mode register (shadowed)
- 0x44 = alternating checkerboard
- 0xFF01 = transfer command
- But there's a question: should we add any initialization registers (like 0x08 for output format, 0x05 for clock divide, etc.)?

Actually, looking at the AD9643 datasheet, key registers are:
- 0x00: chip_port_config (LSB first, soft reset, etc.)
- 0x01: chip_id
- 0x05: clock_divide
- 0x08: output_mode (offset binary vs two's complement, output format)
- 0x0D: test_mode (user test patterns)
- 0xFF: device_update (transfer)
- 0x14: output_adjust

Wait, the AD9643 default output format is offset binary. The code uses signed() which means two's complement. If the ADC outputs offset binary by default, the data will be misinterpreted. However, looking at the AD9643 datasheet, the default is actually offset binary, and many scope applications use offset binary.

But actually, for the default mode, the AD9643 outputs offset binary by default. The dataAd/dataBd are signed(13 downto 0). If we're treating offset binary as signed, that would be wrong - the MSB needs to be inverted to convert offset binary to two's complement.

However, looking deeper at the AD9643, register 0x14 bit 6 controls output format (0=offset binary, 1=two's complement). So the ADC could be configured for two's complement output.

Key design decisions for the clean version:
1. No ETS, no LUT delay line, no digital pattern gen, no AWG triggers
2. 14-bit ADC data throughout
3. AD9643 SPI: proper initialization sequence with checkerboard test, calibration, and return to normal mode
4. TEST_FRAME_MODE = False

7. Remove all unused signals

Let me create the file. I'll make it comprehensive with detailed comments.

For the AD9643 control, the proper sequence is:
1. Write 0x0D44 (test pattern register + checkerboard data) to shadow register
2. Write 0xFF01 (transfer command) to activate checkerboard
3. Perform IDELAY calibration for CH1 (read_calib_start, read_calib_source='0')
4. Write 0xFF01 (transfer) for CH1 cal
5. Perform IDELAY calibration for CH2 (read_calib_start, read_calib_source='1')
6. Write 0xFF01 (transfer) for CH2 cal
7. Write 0x0D00 (test pattern off) to shadow register
8. Write 0xFF01 (transfer) to return to normal sampling

This is already what the code does, so I'll keep and comment it well.

- `trig_level/trig_level_d/trig_level_dd`: `SIGNED(9 downto 0)` - 10-bit level

| 通路 | 信号 | 位宽 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **向外数据** | `adc_data_rise/fall` | 14-bit | ADC原始采样 |

| | `trig_level_r_dd/f_dd` | 10-bit | 带回差门限 |

数据通路全程14-bit → 打包32-bit → DDR3 → FX3 → PC，触发比较始终使用14-bit采样数据的高10位 (`13 downto 4`)，**符合"向外的信号14bit、触发信号10bit"的设计要求**。

3. The adc_if module was updated to handle 14-bit DDR

Now the user is saying this modification caused "serious errors". Let me think about what specific issues could arise:

1. The mavg internal accumulator: With DATA_WIDTH=14 and mavg_len=4, r_acc is 16 bits signed. The max value for 4 samples of 14-bit unsigned is 4*16383=65532 which doesn't fit in signed 16-bit (max 32767). If the ADC outputs offset binary (0 to 16383), the mavg will overflow!

But if the ADC outputs two's complement (-8192 to 8191), then 4*8191=32764 which fits in signed 16-bit (32767).

2. The sample encoder packs 14+14+4=32 bits. Previously it might have been 10+10+12=32 bits (with the 12 digital bits). The PC software needs to know the new format.

3. The trigger comparison uses `dataAd(13 downto 4)` = 10 bits from 14-bit data. If the old version used 10-bit ADC data directly, this would give different scaling.

I think the key question is: what does the PC software expect? And is the AD9643 configured for two's complement or offset binary output?

The "serious error" is probably related to the mavg component's internal accumulator overflow. Let me verify:

The mavg component has:
```vhdl
signal r_acc : signed(DATA_WIDTH+MAX_MAVG_LEN_LOG-1 downto 0);
```

With DATA_WIDTH=14 and MAX_MAVG_LEN_LOG=2:
- r_acc is signed(14+2-1 downto 0) = signed(15 downto 0) = 16 bits, range [-32768, 32767]

When i_data_en='1', the accumulator is:
```vhdl
r_acc <= r_acc + signed(i_data) - p_mavg(p_mavg'length-1);
```

With 14-bit offset binary data (0 to 16383): max accumulated value = 4*16383 = 65532 > 32767 → **OVERFLOW!**

With 14-bit two's complement data (-8192 to 8191): max accumulated value = 4*8191 = 32764 ≤ 32767 → OK.

So if the AD9643 outputs offset binary (DEFAULT), the mavg **will overflow** for large input values. This is the "serious error"!

The fix is to ensure:
1. Either convert offset binary to two's complement before the mavg (invert MSB)
2. Or widen the mavg accumulator to 17 bits (DATA_WIDTH+MAX_MAVG_LEN_LOG = 14+2 = 16, but needs 17 for unsigned)

Actually, let me reconsider. The mavg does `r_acc + signed(i_data) - oldest_sample`. The worst case for accumulated value with offset binary:
- All 4 samples at max (16383): acc = 16383+16383+16383+16383 = 65532

But the internal representation uses `signed`. When you do `signed(i_data)` on a 14-bit offset binary value like 16383 (0x3FFF), it becomes -1 in two's complement. So:

Actually, let me trace through more carefully.

If i_data = "11111111111111" (16383 in unsigned, -1 in signed):
- `signed(i_data)` = -1
- r_acc = -1 + -1 + -1 + -1 = -4 → no overflow in signed arithmetic, but the values are WRONG

If i_data = "10000000000000" (8192 in unsigned, -8192 in signed):
- `signed(i_data)` = -8192
- r_acc accumulates negative values → this would give very wrong results

The fundamental issue is that the AD9643 outputs offset binary by default, but `SIGNED()` treats it as two's complement. This makes ALL values above 8191 appear as negative numbers.

The fix is to convert offset binary to two's complement by inverting the MSB:
```vhdl
-- Convert offset binary to two's complement: invert MSB
dataAd <= SIGNED(adc_data_rise(13) & NOT adc_data_rise(12) & adc_data_rise(11 downto 0));
-- or equivalently:
dataAd <= SIGNED(adc_data_rise) XOR "10000000000000";  -- flip MSB
```

Wait, actually the simpler way is:
```vhdl
dataAd <= SIGNED('0' & adc_data_rise(12 downto 0)) - 8192;
```

Or if using VHDL's `signed`:
```vhdl
signal temp : signed(13 downto 0);
temp <= signed(adc_data_rise);
dataAd <= temp - to_signed(8192, 14);
```

But actually, the most common approach for offset binary to two's complement is to just invert the MSB:
```vhdl
dataAd <= signed(not adc_data_rise(13) & adc_data_rise(12 downto 0));
```

Let me verify: If adc_data_rise = "10000000000000" (8192 mid-scale):
- MSB inverted: "00000000000000" (0) → correct mid-scale in two's complement ✓

If adc_data_rise = "00000000000000" (0):
- MSB inverted: "10000000000000" (-8192) → correct minimum ✓

If adc_data_rise = "11111111111111" (16383):
- MSB inverted: "01111111111111" (8191) → correct maximum ✓

This is the fix! But it should ideally be done in adc_if or at the point where data enters the processing pipeline.

Actually, let me also consider: the AD9643 CAN be configured for two's complement output via register 0x14 bit 6. If we add the SPI configuration for that, we wouldn't need the MSB inversion in the data path.

AD9643 register 0x14 (Output Adjust):
- Bit 6: Output Format (0 = offset binary, 1 = two's complement)

So we can add a SPI write to set register 0x14 bit 6 during initialization.

But even better, let me present both options to the user:
1. Add AD9643 register 0x14 config to set two's complement output (cleaner, in SPI init)
2. Convert offset binary to two's complement in RTL (invert MSB)

The mavg overflow issue is directly caused by this format mismatch. If we fix the format, the mavg overflow goes away.

- mavg 累加器 4×16383=65532 > `signed(15:0)` 最大 32767 → **溢出**

Since elsif checks higher values first and Timer_cnt counts up monotonically, they match sequentially. But the code uses `elsif` which means order matters. Let me verify - the elsif chain has:

```vhdl
if Timer_cnt = 500 then          -- checked first (highest priority)
elsif Timer_cnt = 40500 then     -- ...
...
elsif Timer_cnt = 2000 then      -- checked before 1100 and 1000
elsif Timer_cnt = 1100 then      -- checked after 2000
elsif Timer_cnt = 1000 then      -- checked last
```

Since the values are unique (500, 40500, 40100, 40000, 30100/30101, 30000/30001, 20100/20101, 20000/20001, 2100, 2000, 1100, 1000), the order doesn't matter for functionality - only ONE condition will match at any given Timer_cnt value. ## 修复总结

| 问题 | 根因 | 修复 |
|---|---|---|
| **mavg 累加器溢出** | AD9643 默认偏移二进制，`SIGNED()` 误读为补码，4×16383=65532 > signed_16_max(32767) | 初始化时写 AD9643 寄存器 0x14 bit6=1，设输出为补码 |
| **数据极性反转** | 偏移二进制中点 0x2000→被读成 -8192 | 补码输出后中点=0x0000，`SIGNED()` 正确解析 |
| **触发比较错误** | 数据极性错导致触发门限无意义 | 补码输出后 `dataAd(13 downto 4)` 的高10位正确反映信号幅度 |

Let me now look at the full MST_G state to understand the slwr control flow. The key issue the user mentioned is about the slwr signal. Let me trace the logic:

In the FX3 GPIF II synchronous slave FIFO interface:
- `slwr` is active LOW
- When `slwr_i = '0'`, data is written to FX3 on the rising edge of `clk_fx3` (= `NOT(ifclk)`)
- `flagd` (EP6 full flag) is active LOW — `flagd = '0'` means EP6 is FULL, so we CAN'T write
- `slwr_assert` tracks whether EP6 is ready to accept data

Let me look at the slwr control more carefully. In MST_G:

1. When DDR3 has no data (first branch):
   ```vhdl
   slwr_i <= '0';  -- still assert slwr!
   ```

2. When EP6 is writable (second branch):
   ```vhdl
   slwr_i <= '0';  -- assert slwr
   ```

3. When waiting for data (third branch):
   ```vhdl
   slwr_i <= '1';  -- deassert slwr
   ```

The problem could be:
- In the "no data" branch, slwr is still asserted (`'0'`) even though there's no data to write. This might cause FX3 to read garbage.
- The DMA buffer management with `slwr_assert_cnt` might not properly account for the 1024-byte buffer boundaries.

- Active high means: flagd='0' → EP6 NOT full (can write), flagd='1' → EP6 is FULL (can't write)

- flagd='1' → EP6 is NOT full

Let me look at the slwr control flow more carefully:

In the "EP6 ready and has data to send" branch:
```vhdl
elsif slwr_assert = '1' AND (...conditions...) then
    slwr_i <= '0';  -- assert write strobe
    ...
    if slwr_assert_cnt = (FX3_DMA_BUFFER_SIZE/4) - 1 then
        slwr_assert <= '0';  -- DMA buffer full, stop
        flagd_rdy_cnt <= 0;
        slwr_assert_cnt <= 0;
    else
        slwr_assert_cnt <= slwr_assert_cnt + 1;
    end if;
```

FX3_DMA_BUFFER_SIZE = 1024 bytes. /4 = 256 32-bit words per DMA buffer.

So after writing 256 words, slwr_assert goes to '0', which means it falls through to the else branch which waits for flagd to be re-asserted.

The problem might be that `slwr_assert` stays '0' too long or the flagd check doesn't work correctly. Or maybe the problem is in how slwr is deasserted between bursts.

- The first condition `slwr_assert = '1'` is false

Actually, let me look at the GPIF timing. The FX3 GPIF II operates as follows:
- clk_fx3 = NOT(ifclk) — the FX3 samples on the rising edge of clk_fx3, which is the falling edge of ifclk
- slwr is sampled by FX3 on the rising edge of clk_fx3
- When slwr is low on the rising edge of clk_fx3, data is latched

So our ifclk domain logic:
- On rising_edge(ifclk): we set slwr_i and fdata
- FX3 sees these on the NEXT rising edge of clk_fx3 (= falling edge of ifclk)

This gives half an ifclk cycle of setup time. That should be fine.

Let me look for another issue. When the DMA buffer is full (slwr_assert_cnt = 255):
```vhdl

This takes effect on the NEXT clock. On the next clock, slwr_assert='0', and the process falls to the else branch where slwr_i <= '1'. So slwr is asserted for 256 cycles, then deasserted.

But wait - on the clock where slwr_assert_cnt = 255, we set slwr_assert <= '0'. But we also set `slwr_assert_cnt <= 0` and continue with slwr_i <= '0'. So the 256th write happens. Then next clock, slwr_i goes to '1'. This is correct.

- Asserts `slwr_i <= '0'` for active data transfer
- Counts `slwr_assert_cnt` from 0 to 255

Now, the critical issue: **`flagd_dd = '1'` logic**.

flagd is EP6 full flag. In FX3 GPIF II:
- The flag can be configured as active LOW or active HIGH
- Typically for IN endpoint (EP6): flag is "buffer full" active low → `flagd='0'` = FULL, `flagd='1'` = NOT FULL

But our comment says: `flagd : in STD_LOGIC; -- EP6满标志(低=可写)`

"低=可写" means LOW = writable. This implies:
- `flagd='0'` → EP6 is writable (NOT full)
- `flagd='1'` → EP6 is NOT writable (FULL)

The code checks: `flagd_dd = '1'` → `slwr_assert <= '1'`

If "低=可写" is correct: `flagd='0'` = writable, so we should check `flagd_dd = '0'`. The code is **INVERTED**!

If standard convention is correct: `flagd='0'` = FULL, so `flagd='1'` = writable. The code is correct!

The comment and the code contradict each other. One of them is wrong. Given that the user says there's a "slwr信号问题", and the comment says "低=可写" while the code checks for '1', the most likely issue is that **the flagd polarity is wrong**.

Let me also look at flaga. The comment says: `flaga : in STD_LOGIC; -- EP2空标志(低=非空)`
"低=非空" = LOW = NOT empty → `flaga='0'` = EP2 has data.

The code checks: `if (flaga_d = '1')` → EP2 is NOT empty. This means the code treats `flaga='1'` as "EP2 has data". But the comment says `flaga='0'` means "EP2 has data". Another contradiction!

But wait, for flagd, the comment says "低=可写" (low=writable). If that's the hardware convention, then:
- flagd='0' → writable (NOT full)
- flagd='1' → NOT writable (FULL)

Code checks `flagd_dd = '1'` → `slwr_assert <= '1'` → allows writing. But flagd='1' means FULL! So writing is allowed when FULL — this would cause data loss/corruption!

This IS the "slwr信号问题"! The flagd polarity is inverted in the code!

The fix should be: change `flagd_dd = '1'` to `flagd_dd = '0'`, OR if the comment is wrong and the hardware convention is standard (flagd='0'=FULL), then the comment needs to be fixed.

文件已恢复到原始半重构状态（1598行），核心逻辑如下：

---

### 📡 数据流

```
AD9643(14bit LVDS) → adc_if(IBUFDS+IDELAY+IDDR) → adc_data_rise/fall
  → (可选)mavg 4样点移动平均 → dataAd/dataBd(signed 14bit)
    → sample_encoder(32bit打包) → DDR3DataIn → RAM_DDR3(MIG)
      → DataOut(32bit) → FX3 EP6 → USB → PC
```

---

### ⏱️ 两个进程 / 两个时钟域

| 进程 | 时钟 | 功能 |
|---|---|---|
| `ADC_interface_rising` | `clk_adc_dclk` (250MHz) | 上电复位、ADC数据处理、配置解码、触发检测、采样状态机(ADC_A~F) |
| `FX3_interface` | `ifclk` (~100MHz) | PLL监控、FX3标志同步、帧就绪检测、主状态机(A~G)、GPIF时序 |

---

### 🔄 主状态机 (MasterState, ifclk域)

| 状态 | 功能 |
|---|---|
| **A** | 上电初始化：顺序写 AD9643 SPI(棋盘格→CH1 IDELAY校准→CH2校准→关测试→DAC上电) |
| **B** | 分发器：决策路由(读配置/ADC SPI/DAC SPI/等帧) |
| **C** | 读配置：从 FX3 EP2 读 32 个 32-bit 配置字 → config_RAM |
| **D** | ADC SPI：写 AD9643 寄存器 |
| **E** | DAC SPI：顺序写 MAX5501 四通道(增益/偏置) |
| **F** | 等待帧：向 ADC 域发送 `requestFrame`，等 `frame_ready_to_send` |
| **G** | 流式发送：帧头(256字) + DDR3 读出数据 → FX3 EP6 |

---

### 🎯 采样状态机 (GetSampleState, clk_adc_dclk域)

| 状态 | 功能 |
|---|---|
| **ADC_A** | 空闲，等 `getnewframe` 脉冲，初始化时基与计数器 |
| **ADC_B** | 预触发采集，填满 `pre_trigger` 个样本 |
| **ADC_C** | 等待触发，监测触发条件(电平+斜率+自动超时) |
| **ADC_D** | 触发确认，带回差验证防噪声误触发 |
| **ADC_E** | 后触发采集，采满 `framesize` 个样本 |
| **ADC_F** | Holdoff 等待，强制两次触发最小间隔 |

---

### 🔑 slwr 控制逻辑 (状态 G)

```
Branch 1: flaga_d='1' AND DDR3无数据 AND dword_cnt=0
  → slwr_i <= '1' (停止写), 等7拍 → 回 MST_B

Branch 2: slwr_assert='1' AND (帧头发送中 OR DDR3有数据 OR 补齐)
  → slwr_i <= '0' (写FX3), 计 slwr_assert_cnt 到255 → slwr_assert清零

Branch 3: slwr_assert='1' AND 数据未就绪
  → slwr_i <= '1' (等待)

Branch 4: else (EP6等待)
  → 等 flagd_rdy_cnt=7 后检查 flagd_dd='1' → 重新置 slwr_assert='1'
```

---

### ⚠️ 当前已知问题

1. **`TEST_FRAME_MODE = True`** — 不使用 ADC，直接向 FX3 发测试计数
2. **flagd 检查 `= '1'`** — 注释说"低=可写"，代码却检查 `'1'`（极性可能反）
3. **`IEEE_PROPOSED.FIXED_PKG`** — 引用但未使用
4. **状态 B `TEST_FRAME_MODE` 检查** — 存在但 TEST_FRAME_MODE 判断可能缺失
5. **External analog trigger** — `an_trig_*` / `AnalogTrigTresh` 信号存在但相关电路未实例化

The issue is that `attribute KEEP` is not a standard VHDL attribute - it's a vendor-specific synthesis attribute. The correct way to declare it is:

```vhdl
attribute keep : string;   -- Xilinx uses string type
-- or
attribute KEEP : boolean;  -- Some tools use boolean
```

But in VHDL, attribute names might be case-sensitive in some tools, and `KEEP` might need to be lowercase `keep` for some simulators/synthesis tools. However, for Xilinx Vivado, `KEEP` (uppercase) is the standard.

The correct syntax for Xilinx Vivado is:
```vhdl
attribute KEEP : string;    -- Vivado uses string "TRUE"/"FALSE"
attribute ASYNC_REG : string;
...
attribute KEEP of signal_name : signal is "TRUE";
attribute ASYNC_REG of signal_name : signal is "TRUE";
```

But in VHDL processes, all signal assignments take effect at the END of the process. So both `slwr_i <= '0'` and `fdata <= X"DDDDDDDD"` happen in the same clock cycle. On the next rising edge of ifclk, slwr_i='0' and fdata=X"DDDDDDDD" are visible.

But clk_fx3 = NOT(ifclk). The FX3 samples slwr and fdata on the rising edge of clk_fx3 (falling edge of ifclk). 

Timing:
1. rising_edge(ifclk): process executes, sets slwr_i='0', fdata=X"DDDDDDDD"
2. Signals take effect shortly after (within ifclk period)
3. falling_edge(ifclk) = rising_edge(clk_fx3): FX3 samples slwr='0', fdata=X"DDDDDDDD" ✓

So the timing should be fine. One ifclk half-cycle of setup time is enough for FX3 GPIF (which has a minimum setup time in the ns range, well within the 5ns half-period of 100MHz).

```
进入 MST_G → slrd_i='1', faddr_i="00", ReadingFrame='1'

  ┌─ 分支1: flaga_d='1' AND DataOutValid='0' AND dword_cnt_i=0
  │   → slwr_i='1' (不写), cnt_dw_stop数7拍 → 回MST_B
  │
  ├─ 分支2: slwr_assert='1' AND (hword<256 OR DataOutValid='1' OR ...)
  │   → slwr_i='0' (写FX3) → 先发帧头256字 → 再发DDR3数据
  │   → slwr_assert_cnt计满255 → slwr_assert清零 → 等flagd
  │
  ├─ 分支3: slwr_assert='1' AND 数据等待中
  │   → slwr_i='1' (暂停)
  │
  └─ 分支4: else → 等7拍flagd_dd='1' → slwr_assert='1'
      → slwr_i='1' (暂停)
```

代码逻辑看起来是正确的。EP6返回0字节的可能原因：

| 可能原因 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| **ifclk未运行** | DDR3未校准 → MIG不输出ui_clk → FX3_proc不走 → slwr永远不变 |
| **MST_G从未进入** | 路径: MST_A→B→F→G。MST_F等`frame_ready_to_send`，需要ADC触发。TEST_FRAME_MODE下需auto_trigger超时才触发 |
| **flaga_d阻塞** | 分支1检查`flaga_d='1'`。若FX3固件将flaga配置为高有效(`'1'`=EP2非空)且PC发完配置后flaga保持高，则MST_G一进去就被分支1踢回MST_B |
| **slwr_assert初始化** | 初始值`'1'`正确，但若曾被清零且flagd重确认失败，会永久卡在分支4 |

**最可能的原因**：进入MST_G后，若此时 `flaga_d='1'`（EP2有待读配置），分支1立即命中→停止slwr→回MST_B→再读配置→再回到MST_F等待帧→循环。

With 14-bit data: signed 14-bit range -8192 to 8191. r_acc is 16 bits (range -32768 to 32767). 4*8191 = 32764. This fits! So 14-bit actually FIXES the overflow issue.

```

```

Let me just give a clear answer about the bit layout.数据位的打包格式没有错位问题。逐一验证：

## 14-bit 数据通路位布局

```
ADC原始 → adc_data_rise[13:0] (14bit)
  → dataAd[13:0] signed(14bit)
    → sample_encoder 打包:  [31:18]=CH1  [17:4]=CH2  [3:0]=0000
      → DDR3DataIn[31:0] → DDR3 → DataOut[31:0] → fdata[31:0] → FX3 EP6
```

## 控制/状态信号不受影响

| 信号 | 位宽 | 14bit改动是否影响 |
|---|---|---|
| `slwr_i`, `slrd_i`, `faddr_i` | 1~2 bit | ❌ 无关 |
| `MasterState`, `GetSampleState` | 3~4 bit | ❌ 无关 |
| `hword_cnt_i`, `dword_cnt_i` | integer | ❌ 无关 |
| `trig_signal` | `dataAd(13 downto 4)` 取高10bit | ✅ 10bit触发不变 |
| `fdata` | 32bit | ✅ 14+14+4=32 刚好填满 |

**不会错位**。触发始终用高10bit、数据始终14+14打包32bit，控制信号位宽与数据宽度无关。

### 在 VS Code 中

%APPDATA%\Code\User\workspaceStorage\<workspace-id>\GitHub.copilot-chat\debug-logs\
